00266: def textile(name,base,patterned=False):
00267:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00268:     mat.diffuse_color=(*base,1)
00269:     mat.use_nodes=True
00270:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00271:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00272:     shader=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00273:     shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value=(*base,1)
00274:     shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.9
00275:     coordinates=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00276:     noise=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00277:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value=180
00278:     noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2
00279:     links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"],noise.inputs["Vector"])
00280:     bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00281:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=.17
00282:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.009
00283:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00284:     links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],shader.inputs["Normal"])
00285:     if patterned:
00286:         separate=nodes.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ")
00287:         links.new(coordinates.outputs["Generated"],separate.inputs[0])
00288:         multiply=nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")

00330: def mesh_object(name,vertices,faces,material,parent=group):
00331:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00332:     mesh.from_pydata(vertices,[],faces)
00333:     mesh.update()

00337:     ob.parent=parent

00339:         polygon.use_smooth=True
00340:     return ob
00341: 
00342: def signed_power(value,power):
00343:     return math.copysign(abs(value)**power,value)
00344: 
00345: vertices=[]
00346: faces=[]
00347: segments=96
00348: rings=40
00349: for j in range(rings+1):
00350:     latitude=-math.pi/2+math.pi*j/rings
00351:     radial=max(0,math.cos(latitude))**.36
